HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"blocked","docket_ready":false}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000256/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_38322b38621f848fc39f","text":"2. **Polishing sight before guidance.** Evidence: `ج ل و` B002 (polishing/clearing sight), activated by 92:12 guidance and 92:20 “face”/orientation. Structural cue: day’s manifestation precedes “upon Us is guidance.” Mechanism: light clears the faculty that guidance will direct. Abductive move: I infer that disclosure is a preparatory act, not the whole guidance itself. Reading change: 92:2 becomes a clearing of perception before the moral road is offered.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001559/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_3a0a64ce75b0481333ac","text":"1. **Disclosure as moral legibility.** `ٱلنَّهَار` anchors `ن ه ر:B002` (day opening with light), and `تَجَلَّىٰ` anchors `ج ل و:B001` (uncovering and manifestation) and `ج ل و:B007` (daylight spreading away darkness). Its exact reversal of the covering in 92:1 supplies the physical reveal; the divergent endeavor of 92:4 and the opposed conduct in 92:5–7 supply what becomes distinguishable. The directional arrow from illumination to moral diagnosis is inferred, not stated. Day therefore does more than replace night: it makes otherwise hidden differences in striving readable.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000256/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_43c21e85a96c94d36041","text":"92:4–7 supplied the unexpected object and use of disclosure: divergent striving becomes legible and then one course becomes ready. In particular, 92:7 changed `ج ل و:B002` from optical clearing into practical route-preparation; that enabling arrow remains inferred.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000256/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_7797e58e205d825ffee7","text":"3. **Bridal display under creation and gift imagery.** Evidence: `ج ل و` B003 (bridal display), B009 (bridal presentation gift), activated by 92:3 male/female, `ص د ق` B007 bridal dower in 92:6, and gift-seeking in 92:20. Structural cue: unveiling follows sexed creation and precedes giving. Mechanism: display/gift imagery makes “manifestation” social and covenantal, not merely optical. Abductive move: I infer that exposure can establish relation and obligation. Reading change: day’s unveiling can imply accountable presentation before the Lord, not bare daylight.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001559/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_845c4b46295c430dec6b","text":"1. **Day as opening and clarification.** Evidence: `ن ه ر` B002 (day opening with light), `ج ل و` B001 (uncovering/manifestation), B007 (daylight spreading). Structural cue: it answers 92:1 with exact reversal: cover gives way to manifestation. Mechanism: disclosure transforms hidden striving into legible trajectories. Abductive move: I supply the causal arrow that light reveals not only objects but consequences. Reading change: the day is an epistemic event: the split in 92:4 becomes visible.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000256/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_b753c9a7c665c50db0b2","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"92:2:3\",\"root_000256\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Bu açılma, yalnızca görünürlük sağlamaz; ileride gösterilecek yolda hareket edebilmek için görüşü temizleyen bir hazırlık işlevi de görür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000256/B001","root_000256/B007","root_001559/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_dad5d2d49ded1d79ad2f","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"92:2:1\",\"root_001559\",[\"B002\"]],[\"92:2:3\",\"root_000256\",[\"B001\",\"B007\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Gündüzün açılması, örtülü kalan farklı çabaları ve onların sonuçlarını ahlaken okunabilir hâle getiren bir açıklık kurar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000256/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_e78855f4dbfd826fe3d5","text":"2. **Clearing that prepares a route.** `ج ل و:B002` contributes polishing or clearing sight, while 92:4 supplies purposeful endeavor (`س ع ي`) and 92:7 repeats readiness/ease (`ي س ر`). Their sequence—clearing, divergent movement, then facilitation—supports a mechanism in which visibility enables directed movement. The packet gives the order and images; the claim that clarified sight prepares a course is abductive.
